# Why the base is a fixed commit and not `origin/master`. `origin` in this clone is
# upstream LMMS/lmms; this repo never pushes to it, and its `master` ref tracks whatever
# upstream publishes - on 2026-09-11 it was 518a7e8ef, which is NOT an ancestor of the
# fork point (verified: `git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/master 4e677cb6c6ab` -> 1).

# 4e677cb6c6ab (`git cat-file -e 4e677cb6c6ab:<path>`). The fork point is a fixed commit,
# so the corrected command needs no remote state at all. Verify it is in the clone first:
#   git cat-file -e 4e677cb6c6ab^{commit} && echo ok
# (`git merge-base origin/master HEAD` returns the same commit - 4e677cb6c6ab - but only
# while origin has been fetched; the literal commit is the base that cannot drift.)

# eighteen siblings: it is a control-surface test script under tests/, admitted by
# name rather than by widening the extension filter. It is the registered ctest
# that makes the MCP bridge's committed offline snapshot
# (tools/mcp-zene-control/zene_control/commands_snapshot.json) a measured artifact:
# it starts the built binary with --control-socket, asks it for
# control.commands_list, loads the snapshot through the bridge's OWN loader
# (zene_control.registry.load_bundle) and fails on drift in either direction,
# naming the ids and the regeneration command. Registered here rather than left
# outside every scope because the two ratchets it widens were re-measured with it
# registered and pass (Gate 4: max CCN 8; Gate 7: 254 lines), and no baseline was
# re-anchored for either. Reproduces verified with the command under "Verify it"
# after the entry was committed.

# stable-ids, mmpz-git-depth, automation-modes, controller-surfaces, mpe-playback,
# code9-named-pipe). Six entries arrived here that this file's own command cannot
# produce, resolved by this file's recorded rule rather than by widening a ratchet:

#     tests/src/core/ControllerSurfaceTest.cpp,

#     reason). Nothing is unregistered by the move: Gate 9 accepts either registration,

#   * THREE python files (tests/control-named-pipe-smoke.py, tests/control_pipe_client.py,
#     tests/control-stable-ids-slice2.py) have no home in tests/all-sources.txt, which
#     lists C/C++ sources only. Each is a proof/library of a fork-NEW feature (the
#     Windows named-pipe transport, its pipe client, and the stable-id slice-2 proof), so
#     this is their scope; every one of their names is added to EVERY python pathspec
#     line of both blocks above, which is what makes the recipe derive them.
#   * the merge also left the ENTRY LIST OUT OF ORDER (the merge machinery splices each
#     side's appended block at that side's insertion point), which this file's verify
#     command reports as a failure because it diffs the file's own order against a
#     sorted emission. The list was re-emitted in byte order with every comment and blank
#     line left where it was - same entry set, same slots, sorted contents.

# files of the real-time-safety whole-tree sweep - rt-safety-sweep.py (the gate),
# rt_safety_selftest.py (its controls) and the two modules they share (rt_safety_source.py:
# the rules and the source reader; rt_safety_lib.py: the scope and allowlist ledgers, the
# sweep and its report). The pathspec line above is the fourth of the file's named additions
# and it is what makes the recipe derive them; nothing else in the list changed, no file that
# was already registered is affected, and the sweep's two DATA files
# (tests/rt-safety-scope.txt, tests/rt-safety-allowlist.txt) are not sources by extension and
# have no home here - they are the sweep's own ledgers, read at run time, not measured code.
# Measured before registering: every function in the four files is CCN <= 10 (lizard), every
# file is under 500 lines, so the fork ratchets gates 4 and 7 measure them at their existing
# thresholds rather than moving a baseline.
# 2026-09-22 (lane 040/ui-precond, UI precondition #1): two fork-NEW headers enter here -
# include/Accessibility.h (the announce/describe pattern every later accessibility sweep
# copies) and include/WcagContrast.h (the WCAG contrast helper of SPEC-zene-ui-v0 §4.5,
# lower in the file under W). Both are header-only; measured before registering, every
# function is CCN <= 3 and both are far under 500 lines, so gates 4 and 7 measure them at
# their thresholds. The two tests that exercise them live in tests/src/gui/, which the
# recipe's pathspec does not cover - the FocusDesk tests sit the same way, registered in
# tests/CMakeLists.txt and listed in tests/all-sources.txt.
